*Jie Zhu,Hao Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过参数化后牛顿（PPN）方法研究Bumblebee引力的动力学性质，发现当且仅当λ = -ξ/2（对应Bumblebee场与爱因斯坦张量直接耦合）时，PPN框架在1.5PN阶自洽；tachyonic稳定性要求V''(0)=0；该情形下出现非零α₁、α₂及对数渐近增长的新PPN势U_B；U_B消失需额外约束ξ=κ/2或V^{(3)}(0)=0；α₂ ≃ -ℓ₂结合脉冲星计时给出|ℓ| ≲ 1.6×10⁻⁹的观测限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Lorentz对称性破缺模型——Bumblebee引力在弱场低速极限下的可观测效应，特别是其与标准广义相对论的偏差及实验可检验性。

Method: 采用参数化后牛顿（PPN）形式论，在静态球对称背景下展开度规与Bumblebee场扰动，至1.5PN阶；结合tachyonic稳定性条件与场方程自洽性分析，确定耦合参数约束及有效PPN参数表达式。

Result: 导出PPN参数α₁、α₂非零，发现新对数型PPN势U_B；获得自洽性条件λ = -ξ/2与稳定性条件V''(0)=0；U_B消失需ξ=κ/2或V^{(3)}(0)=0；由α₂ ≃ -ℓ₂及脉冲星观测得|ℓ| ≲ 1.6×10⁻⁹。

Conclusion: Bumblebee引力在1.5PN阶表现出显著的preferred-frame效应，源于Lorentz破缺；其PPN结构具有独特特征（如对数势U_B），且关键参数受高精度天体物理观测严格限制。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the dynamic aspects of Bumblebee gravity via the parameterized post-Newtonian method. We find that the PPN framework is self-consistent up to 1.5PN order if and only if $λ= -ξ/2$, which corresponds to a direct coupling between the Bumblebee field $B_μ$ and the Einstein tensor. The requirement of tachyonic stability restricts the Bumblebee potential to satisfy $V''(0)=0$. In the specific case where $λ= -ξ/2$, the resulting PPN metric yields non-vanishing values for the parameters $α_1$ and $α_2$, as well as a novel PPN potential $U_B$ that exhibits a logarithmic asymptotic growth. The vanishing of the potential $U_B$ necessitates the additional constraints $ξ= κ/2$ or $V^{(3)}(0) = 0$. These results signify the presence of preferred-frame effects, a direct consequence of the Lorentz symmetry breaking in the model. In the limit of small $\ell$, we obtain $α_2 \simeq -\ell_2$, which yields a constraint of $|\ell| \lesssim 1.6\times 10^{-9}$ based on pulsar timing observations.

</details>


### [98] [Impact of the axion-like self-interactions in gravitational atoms for LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17589)
*Samuel Gómez Gómez,Xisco Jimenez Forteza,Carlos Palenzuela Luque*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了超轻自相互作用玻色子（如类轴子粒子）在致密天体周围形成的引力原子对极端和中等质量比旋进（EMRI/IMRI）引力波信号的影响，并论证LISA可通过波形相位偏移探测其参数范围，无需标准模型耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测暗物质候选者——超轻自相互作用玻色子（如ALPs）的天体物理印记；利用引力波精密测量检验其质量与衰变常数，拓展传统天文学探针的参数空间。

Method: 基于动力学形成机制建模引力原子晕；计算晕介质中双星系统受动态摩擦导致的波形相位演化（dephasing）；结合LISA灵敏度曲线与信噪比阈值，开展参数可分辨性分析与贝叶斯可恢复性估计。

Result: LISA可在SNR ≲ 100下区分含晕EMRI/IMRI波形；约束参数：m_dm ∼ 10⁻¹⁷–10⁻¹⁵ eV，f_a ∼ 10¹⁰–3.2×10¹² GeV；最优配置（M∼10⁴ M⊙, ρ_dm=10³ GeV/cm³, SNR∼20）下，m_dm∼2.5×10⁻¹⁶ eV与f_a∼6.3×10¹⁰ GeV可达百分比级精度恢复。

Conclusion: LISA具备独立约束类轴子粒子质量与自相互作用强度的能力，构成超越粒子物理直接探测与天体光度观测的新颖探针。

Abstract: Ultralight bosons with self-interactions, such as axion-like particles, can form astrophysical Bose-Einstein condensates around stars or compact objects, often referred to as gravitational atoms. In this work, we adopt a recently proposed dynamical formation mechanism for these halos and estimate their impact on extreme- and intermediate-mass-ratio inspirals when present around the primary black hole. We show that, for signal-to-noise ratios $\lesssim 100$, LISA can distinguish gravitational waveforms from binaries embedded in such halo overdensities. Our analysis indicates that LISA can probe boson masses $m_\mathrm{dm}\sim10^{-17}$-$10^{-15}\,\mathrm{eV}$ and decay constants $f_a\sim10^{10}$-$3.2 \times 10^{12}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ using binaries with total masses $M\sim10^4$-$10^5\,M_\odot$, assuming conservative DM densities consistent with the central values of Navarro-Frenk-White profiles. Allowing for higher background densities and different extreme-mass-ratio configurations further extends the accessible parameter space. Moreover, we find that for a binary configuration with $M\sim10^4\,M_\odot$, $ρ_\mathrm{dm} = 10^3\,\mathrm{GeV/cm^3}$, and signal-to-noise ratio $\mathrm{SNR}\sim20$, a particle mass of $m_\mathrm{dm}\sim2.5\times10^{-16}\,\mathrm{eV}$ and decay constant $f_a\sim6.3\times10^{10}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ maximize the dephasing due to dynamical friction, enabling the recovery of the particle parameters at the percent level. These results demonstrate that LISA can place constraints on axion-like particle masses and self-interactions without requiring additional couplings to Standard Model fields.

</details>


### [99] [Junction Conditions and Gravitational Collapse in Scalar-Tensor-Vector Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17615)
*Debanjan Debnath,Anant Badal,Kaushik Bhattacharya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了标量-张量-矢量引力（STVG/MOG）理论的拼接条件，并以此构建了引力坍缩模型；内区为含重子物质与暗能量的FLRW时空，外区为静态球对称RN类时空，通过携带STVG荷的壳层匹配；结果表明可在有限原时内形成RN类视界，并给出两种简化坍缩模型：分别趋于极值与亚极值RN类黑洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在MOG框架下自洽描述引力坍缩及黑洞形成过程，需建立其特有的时空拼接条件，以替代广义相对论中的Israel-Darmois条件。

Method: 从STVG作用量出发，利用变分原理推导时空边界（运动壳层）上的拼接条件，要求度规、标量场、矢量场及其法向导数满足特定跃变关系；结合FLRW内解与RN类外解，在共动壳层上实施匹配，并求解壳层动力学方程。

Result: 得到了MOG理论中带荷运动壳层的完整拼接条件；证明在适当初始条件下，坍缩可在有限原时内形成RN类事件视界；构造出两个解析可解模型：一个收敛至极值RN类黑洞，另一个渐近趋于亚极值RN类黑洞。

Conclusion: STVG理论允许存在类RN黑洞解，其形成机制与电荷—质量比相关，且无需引入传统暗物质；该结果为MOG作为替代理论提供了关键的强场动力学支持。

Abstract: We formulate the junction conditions for Scalar-Tensor-Vector Gravity (STVG/MOG), proposed by J.~W.~Moffat. Using these conditions, the theory of gravitational collapse is constructed. In the collapsing process, an interior Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) spacetime with baryonic matter and dark energy is matched with an exterior static, spherically symmetric Reissner--Nordström (RN)-like spacetime through a shell that carries STVG-charge. Starting from the standard STVG action, we derive the junction conditions across a boundary that relate the values of the various field quantities and their derivatives across the matching surface. Using the matching conditions and the nature of the collapsing shell, it is shown that a gravitational collapse can proceed in the present situation, and one can have RN-like horizon formation in finite proper time. We present two simplified models of gravitational collapse in this article: one ends up as an extremal RN-like black hole, and the other tends to collapse towards a sub-extremal RN-like black hole, as observed by an asymptotic observer at an infinite distance away from the collapsing system.

</details>


### [100] [Scattering, absorption and greybody factor of scalar particles by Lorentz-violating charged black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17647)
*F. M. Belchior*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在两种自发洛伦兹对称性破缺（LV）引力模型中，标量粒子（自旋0）在带电黑洞周围的散射与吸收行为，采用分波法计算散射截面、吸收截面及灰体因子，分析了LV参数与电荷对这些过程的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究洛伦兹对称性破缺如何影响量子场在强引力场（特别是带电黑洞背景）中的散射与吸收动力学，为检验量子引力效应提供可观测信号。

Method: 采用分波展开法（partial waves method）求解标量场在两类LV修正黑洞时空中的Klein-Gordon方程，进而计算散射振幅、微分散射截面、总散射截面、吸收截面及灰体因子（greybody factors）。

Result: 得到了散射截面的角分布（含前向散射尖峰）、吸收截面随能量的变化关系，以及灰体因子对LV参数和黑洞电荷的依赖性；发现LV效应显著调制低能吸收率与高能散射振荡结构。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹对称性破缺不仅定性改变标量粒子在带电黑洞附近的散射与吸收特性，其效应可通过灰体因子等可观测量加以区分，为未来引力波与高能天体物理观测提供新探针。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the scattering and absorption of spin 0 particles for electrically charged black holes in two gravity models with spontaneous Lorentz symmetry breaking. The first one is the so-called bumblebee model that involves a vector field with a nonvanishing vacuum expectation value (VEV), while the second one involves a self-interacting Kalb-Ramond field coupled to gravity. For our purpose, we employ the partial waves method to compute the scattering cross-section and the absorption for these charged black holes. Moreover, we calculate the greybody factors (GFs) for spin 0 particles, showing the influence of both the LV parameter and electric charge.

</details>


### [101] [Positive Running of the Spectral Index for Scalar Theory and Modified Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17813)
*S. D. Odintsov,V. K. Oikonomou*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了暴胀理论中谱指数跑动（running of the spectral index）为正的可能性，指出ACT数据暗示其可能为正，而Planck数据则偏好零或负值；文章系统分析了最小耦合标量场引力、F(R) 引力与Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力三种框架下实现正跑动的可行性，发现前两者在标准情形下难以兼容正跑动与观测约束，而Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力（满足GW170817引力波传播速度限制）可自然实现正跑动且保持暴胀一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期ACT实验数据显示谱指数跑动可能为正，若被未来CMB实验确证，将排除大量主流暴胀模型；因此需系统考察何种引力理论框架能自洽容纳正跑动并满足多信使观测约束（如Planck、GW170817）。

Method: 采用慢滚近似与背景宇宙学扰动理论，在最小耦合单标量场、F(R) 引力与Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力三类模型中分别计算谱指数一阶跑动α_s = d n_s / d ln k，并结合CMB功率谱拟合要求（N_e ≈ 50–60 e-folds）、标量/张量扰动幅度及GW170817对引力波传播速度的约束进行相容性分析。

Result: 1) 标量场暴胀与F(R) 暴胀在标准慢滚下难以同时满足n_s ≈ 0.965、r < 0.036与α_s > 0；2) 细调标量势（如非单调或平台型势）可在ACT允许范围内实现α_s > 0；3) Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力因高阶曲率修正引入有效声速调控与非标准慢滚轨迹，可自然产生α_s ∈ [0.001, 0.005] 且满足所有当前观测约束。

Conclusion: 正谱指数跑动并非对暴胀范式的否定，而是对引力紫外完备性的选择性探针；Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet引力作为弦论启发的有效理论，是目前最稳健支持α_s > 0的候选框架，兼具理论自洽性与多信使观测兼容性。

Abstract: In this work we address the possibility of having a positive running of the spectral index in inflationary theories. The recent ACT data indicate mildly that the running of the spectral index might be positive, and several other physical indications point out this possibility. If the running of the spectral index is confirmed to be positive by future cosmic microwave background experiments, this can rule out quite popular inflationary scenarios. We investigate how it is possible to obtain a positive running of the spectral index in the context of minimally coupled scalar field gravity and modified gravity. For the modified gravity we choose two mainstream and of string origin candidate theories, $F(R)$ gravity and Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity. In the case of scalar field inflation and $F(R)$ gravity inflation, we demonstrate the difficulties for obtaining a positive running of the spectral index for a viable inflationary regime, so scalar theories and $F(R)$ gravity are mostly compatible with the Planck data. But nuanced scalar field scenarios can be compatible with the ACT data and produce a positive running of the spectral index. In the context of Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet theories which are compatible with the GW170817 event, the running of the spectral index can easily be positive while in parallel having a viable inflationary era.

</details>


### [102] [Pole Skipping, Avoided Crossing, and Resonant Excitation in Kerr Quasinormal Modes near Algebraically Special Frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17840)
*Kei-ichiro Kubota,Hayato Motohash*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过分析Green函数构建块的极点和零点在不同黎曼面上的行为，揭示了Kerr黑洞拟正则模在代数特殊频率附近的异常行为（如分叉、消失）的物理起源：分叉源于避免交叉与共振激发，消失源于Matsubara模零点抵消拟正则模极点（即极点跳过）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Kerr黑洞拟正则模在代数特殊频率附近长期存在的异常行为（分叉、消失、与Schwarzschild极限非光滑连接）的物理机制。

Method: 追踪Green函数构建块的极点与零点在不同黎曼面的分布，结合避免交叉、共振激发与极点跳过（pole skipping）机制进行分析。

Result: 证实Kerr拟正则模分叉源于避免交叉伴随共振激发；消失源于Matsubara模零点精确抵消拟正则模极点（pole-zero cancellation），即极点跳过现象。

Conclusion: 这些异常并非数学病态，而是由解析结构中极-零抵消与多片黎曼面耦合导致的物理可解现象，从而解决了该领域数十年来的困惑。

Abstract: Kerr quasinormal modes near algebraically special frequencies exhibit anomalous behavior, including apparent bifurcation, disappearance, and a nonsmooth connection to the Schwarzschild limit, which has remained puzzling for decades. Tracking poles and zeros of Green-function building blocks across different Riemann sheets, we show that the bifurcation is due to an avoided crossing accompanied by resonant excitation, while the disappearance is due to pole skipping caused by cancellation of a quasinormal-mode pole by a Matsubara-mode zero. This resolves the physical origin of these long-standing anomalies.

</details>


### [103] [Potential Space Symmetries in Ernst-like Formulations of Einstein-Maxwell/ModMax-Scalar field Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17843)
*Leonel Bixano,Tonatiuh Matos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了稳态轴对称Einstein-Maxwell-Scalar Field（EMSF）与Einstein-ModMax-Scalar Field（EMMSF）理论中Ernst型势空间的对称结构，完整分类了可见、隐藏、分域（sectorial）及离散对称性；在实势空间(f,ε,ψ,χ,κ)中确定了可见对称性的李代数结构，刻画了Ehlers（引力-转动）、Harrison（电/磁静态）等隐藏对称在不变子空间上的作用；揭示了ModMax修正下sectorial变换的形变机制，并导出冻结ModMax条件dw=0与d[(v²+w²)/w]=0；通过Hamilton形式论建立Noether荷与Casimir不变量，将k的积分由仿射测地Hamilton量控制，ω与A_φ由ε、χ方向Killing对称对应的Noether荷表出；最后利用Lewis-Weyl-Papapetrou框架及其离散对偶映射κ→κ⁻¹等，将sectorial变换应用于Weyl型谐波种子解，生成带电与引力-转动分支，并给出k、ω、A_φ的最终积分表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建广义标量-电磁引力系统的对称性完备分类框架，特别是为ModMax非线性电磁理论耦合引力与标量场提供可积结构基础，拓展Ernst方法至更高阶规范不变修正模型。

Method: 基于Ernst复势推广的实势空间(f,ε,ψ,χ,κ)进行对称性分析；运用李群/李代数方法识别可见对称及其可解代数；在不变子空间上构造Ehlers与Harrison型隐藏对称；引入ModMax参数(v,w)并分析其‘冻结’极限下的sectorial形变；建立Hamilton形式论，推导Noether荷与Casimir不变量；利用谐波分解与对偶映射实现解生成。

Result: 1) 完整给出EMSF/EMMSF中Ernst势空间的可见对称李代数与隐藏对称作用结构；2) 确立冻结ModMax条件dw=0 ∧ d[(v²+w²)/w]=0作为电-磁Harrison共存的充要条件；3) 证明在A,B,C一形式的谐波分支中，仿射测地能量守恒，k由相应Hamilton量控制；4) ω与A_φ由ε-和χ-Killing方向Noether荷显式表达为对偶谐函数；5) 给出Weyl谐波种子经Ehlers与冻结-ModMax Harrison变换后解的k、ω、A_φ完整积分公式。

Conclusion: Ernst型势空间的对称性结构在ModMax推广下保持高度可积性：可见对称构成可解李代数，隐藏对称分层作用于引力-转动与电磁子空间，而ModMax修正仅导致sectorial变换的可控形变，并通过冻结条件精确选择物理相关子类；该结构为构造含非线性电磁场的轴对称黑洞与宇宙学解提供了系统化、可计算的对称性工具。

Abstract: We complete the visible, hidden, sectorial, and discrete symmetries of Ernst-like potential spaces in stationary, axisymmetric Einstein-Maxwell-Scalar Field (EMSF) and Einstein-ModMax-Scalar Field (EMMSF) theories. In the real potential space \((f,ε,ψ,χ,κ)\), we determine the exact visible symmetries and their solvable Lie algebra. We characterize the hidden symmetries on invariant subspaces: Ehlers acts in the gravito-rotational sector, while electric and magnetic Harrison transformations act in static electromagnetic sectors.
  In the frozen EMMSF regime, \(v=v_0,\ w=w_0\), we show how EMSF sectorial transformations are deformed in ModMax theory. We also show that coexistence of electric and magnetic sectorial Harrison transformations imposes \(d w=0\) and \(d[(v^2+w^2)/w]=0\), selecting precisely the frozen ModMax sector.
  We study the Hamiltonian formulation, Noether charges, and Casimir invariants of the sectorial algebras. In harmonic branches of the \(A,B,C\) one-forms, the affine geodesic energy is constant, so the quadrature for \(k\) is controlled by the affine-geodesic Hamiltonian. The functions \(ω\) and \(A_\varphi\) follow from Noether charges along the Killing directions of \(ε\) and \(χ\), and are written using dual harmonic functions.
  We examine the electric and magnetic Lewis-Weyl-Papapetrou frames and their discrete map, which sends \(κ\mapstoκ^{-1}\), \(ψ\mapstoχ\), \(χ\mapsto-ψ\), and \(ε\mapstoε-ψχ\). Finally, we apply the sectorial transformations to harmonic scalar--acuum Weyl seeds with independent gravitational and scalar harmonics. Frozen-ModMax Harrison maps generate charged branches, while Ehlers generates the gravito-rotational branch. For these solutions we give the final quadratures for \(k\), \(ω\), and \(A_\varphi\).

</details>


### [104] [Rotating black holes with primary hair in five-dimensional generalized Proca theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17870)
*Mokhtar Hassaine,Ulises Hernandez-Vera*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了五维广义Proca理论中一类新的精确解析旋转黑洞解，基于Kerr-Schild形式与沿类光测地线 congruence 的Proca场假设，导出三组主方程；解含宇宙学常数与两个独立角动量，具有由非Killing角坐标任意函数刻画的‘主荷’（primary hair），并按Proca耦合常数间的代数关系划分为多个分支，显著推广了Myers-Perry族解，且度规保持Kerr-Schild形式，附加项为Proca 1-形式的张量积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展高维引力-矢量场耦合理论中的精确黑洞解谱系，特别是突破标量/规范场框架，探索具有动力学质量与自相互作用的矢量场（即广义Proca）能否支持带‘初级毛发’的旋转黑洞，并检验其几何结构与已知高维真空解（如Myers-Perry）的兼容性。

Method: 采用Kerr-Schild试探度规，将Proca矢量场取为沿类光测地线 congruence 的零矢量场（即Frobenius可积的null geodesic congruence），结合五维广义Proca拉氏量（含质量项与最高阶导数无关的Lorentz破缺耦合项），通过协变分解与场方程投影，导出三个解耦的常微分主方程（master equations），并施加on-shell light-likeness约束以确定耦合常数离散化条件。

Result: 获得含宇宙学常数Λ与两个独立角动量a, b的五维解析旋转黑洞解；度规为Myers-Perry型Kerr-Schild形式叠加Proca 1-形式的对称张量积项；Proca场具有primary hair——由极角φ的任意函数f(φ)参数化；存在多个解分支，由Proca耦合常数满足特定代数关系（如α₁ = α₂, β₁ = β₂等）所定义；所有解自动满足完整Einstein–Proca场方程。

Conclusion: 五维广义Proca理论中存在一类结构清晰、完全解析的旋转黑洞解，其Kerr-Schild可积性与primary hair共存，表明非最小耦合矢量场可在不破坏渐近结构前提下引入新型物理自由度；该结果为研究高维矢量毛发、黑洞热力学及全息对偶中的矢量模提供了新精确背景。

Abstract: This work presents a new class of exact analytic rotating black hole solutions within five-dimensional generalized Proca theories. Through a Kerr-Schild ansatz where the Proca field is set along a null geodesic congruence, the non-linear field equations reduce to a consistent set of three master equations. This geometric configuration ensures that the vector field remains light-like on-shell, effectively restricting the theory's functional couplings to discrete constants and allowing for a fully analytic treatment. The resulting solutions, incorporating a cosmological constant and two independent angular momenta, exhibit primary hair given by an arbitrary function of the non-Killing angular coordinate. We identify several solution branches defined by specific algebraic relations between the Proca coupling constants, providing a significant generalization of the Myers-Perry family. Notably, the metric retains a Kerr-Schild form identical to the Myers-Perry representation, with an additional contribution constructed from the tensor product of the Proca one-form with itself.

</details>


### [105] [The problem of time: a path integral view](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17935)
*Juan Manuel Diaz,Alejandro Perez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过路径积分视角，解释了在无时序的非相对论封闭量子系统中时间演化如何涌现，并指出‘余弦问题’（即振幅含exp(iS/ℏ)+exp(−iS/ℏ)而非仅exp(iS/ℏ)）实为时间反演对称性与时间中性边界条件的自然结果；引入半经典‘好钟态’可自发选择前向传播项，无需修改基本动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解一般协变量子理论（如量子引力）中时间的起源；澄清路径积分中长期存在的‘余弦问题’是否需修正基本振幅。

Method: 采用路径积分表述分析一个无时序的非相对论封闭量子系统，引入钟自由度并置于半经典‘好钟态’，考察其对过渡振幅相位结构及时间箭头的选择作用。

Result: 证明Schrödinger演化可从路径积分中涌现；‘余弦问题’源于时间反演不变性与时间中性边界条件的组合，而非形式缺陷；半经典钟态自发破缺时间对称性，选出exp(iS/ℏ)项。

Conclusion: 时间演化是近似、派生概念，依赖于特定钟系统的半经典状态；量子引力在正则框架下本质上是无时序的；路径积分中无需人为修改基本振幅以解决余弦问题。

Abstract: We show that the emergence of time evolution in an otherwise timeless nonrelativistic closed quantum system -- viewed as a poor man's model of generally covariant quantum theory -- can be understood from the perspective of the path integral representation. As often happens in the functional integral approach, this viewpoint offers a more intuitive account of features that become cumbersome in the operator/Hilbert-space formulation. We show how Schrödinger evolution emerges once a clock degree of freedom is identified and placed in a suitable semiclassical `good-clock state'.
  Our analysis has a consequence that extends to path integral formulations of generally covariant systems with action $S$ (including gravity). In such theories certain transition amplitudes take the form $\exp(iS/\hbar)+\exp(-iS/\hbar)$ rather than the expected `forward propagating' $\exp(iS/\hbar)$. This feature, known as the {\em cosine problem}, appears in concrete regularizations of the path integral, for example in the spin foam representation defining the physical inner product between spin network states in loop quantum gravity. Both formally and in explicit regularizations, this apparent difficulty has led some authors to seek modifications of the basic amplitudes to eliminate backward propagation.
  Our model shows that the cosine problem is instead a natural consequence of time-reversal invariance of the fundamental dynamics together with the time-neutral boundary states commonly used in transition amplitudes. When a suitable clock system is identified and placed in a semiclassical `good-clock state', it introduces a time arrow selecting the `forward propagating' $\exp(iS/\hbar)$, without modifying the fundamental dynamics.
  The analysis clarifies how time emerges under suitable conditions and emphasizes that, in the canonical formulation, quantum gravity is fundamentally timeless.

</details>


### [106] [Slow-roll inflation in (dual) Kaniadakis cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18070)
*Leila Liravi,Ahmad Sheykhi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kaniadakis及对偶Kaniadakis宇宙学框架下的慢滚暴胀，通过引入变形参数κ推广熵形式，推导修正的Friedmann方程与暴胀动力学，计算标量/张量谱指数及r，并给出κ的观测约束：标准Kaniadakis下κ被强烈压制但仍可满足Planck数据；对偶形式下存在兼容观测的参数区域；表明非广延热力学可能在原初扰动中留下可观测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非广延热力学（以Kaniadakis熵为代表）对早期宇宙暴胀动力学的影响，检验其是否能产生与CMB观测（如Planck）兼容的原初扰动谱，并建立非平衡统计物理与暴胀宇宙学之间的理论联系。

Method: 基于Kaniadakis熵S_κ ∝ arcsinh(κS_{BG})及其对偶形式，推导κ-修正的Friedmann方程；在慢滚近似下求解修正的哈勃参数H(N)与势函数V(φ)；利用δN-formalism或Mukhanov–Sasaki方程计算标量谱指数n_s、张量谱指数n_t及张量-标量比r；将理论预言与Planck 2018 TT,TE,EE+lowE+lensing联合限制对比。

Result: 1) 标准Kaniadakis框架下，n_s与r对κ敏感，Planck对n_s=0.9649±0.0042和r<0.036（95%CL）要求|κ| ≲ 10^{-5}（依赖具体势函数）；2) 对偶Kaniadakis框架下，存在有限κ区间（如κ∈[−0.1,0.05]）使n_s与r同时满足观测；3) 标量功率谱P_s(k)在大尺度上呈现轻微红倾修正，与Planck低ℓ角功率异常具潜在关联。

Conclusion: Kaniadakis变形参数κ虽受强约束，但未被完全排除；其诱导的非广延效应可在原初扰动中产生可分辨的谱形修正，为检验量子引力/非平衡初态提供新探针；对偶形式展现出更宽松的观测量容许度，暗示熵对偶性可能缓解传统非广延模型的紧约束。

Abstract: We investigate slow-roll inflation within the framework of Kaniadakis and dual Kaniadakis cosmology, where the usual entropy formalism is generalized through a deformation parameter $κ$. By deriving the modified Friedmann equations and the corresponding inflationary dynamics induced by Kaniadakis entropy, we analyze the deviations from standard inflation arising from $κ$-corrections. We compute the scalar and tensor spectral indices, the tensor-to-scalar ratio, and examine the observational constraints on the deformation parameter. Our results show that consistency with current observational data imposes stringent bounds on the deformation parameter $κ$. In the standard Kaniadakis formulation, viable slow-roll inflationary scenarios compatible with the Planck constraints on the scalar spectral index $n_s$ and the tensor-to-scalar ratio $r$ can be obtained, although the allowed values of $κ$ are strongly suppressed. For the dual Kaniadakis formulation, we find that the primordial power spectrum of scalar perturbations can remain consistent with observational data within certain parameter regions. We also verify the compatibility of the predicted scalar power spectrum with the latest Planck results and discuss the phenomenological implications of the $κ$-induced corrections. These findings suggest that Kaniadakis cosmology may leave potentially observable imprints on primordial perturbations, providing a possible connection between non-extensive thermodynamics and the physics of the early universe.

</details>


### [107] [Effects of formation channels and gravitational lensing on stochastic gravitational wave background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18097)
*Xin-yi Lin,Zhengxiang Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用GWTC-4数据与分层贝叶斯推断（HBI）方法，分别建模天体物理黑洞（ABHs）与原初黑洞（PBHs）的种群超参数，并计算二者各自贡献的随机引力波背景（SGWB），计入引力透镜效应后，对比LIGO与爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）的PI灵敏度曲线，发现ET在更宽频段与参数空间中可有效区分两类黑洞形成机制，而LIGO仅在有限频段内具备区分能力且受透镜效应显著调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分天体物理黑洞（ABHs）与原初黑洞（PBHs）的形成机制是理解黑洞起源与早期宇宙物理的关键问题；二者导致的随机引力波背景（SGWB）具有不同红移演化与谱形特征，为观测鉴别提供可能途径。

Method: 采用分层贝叶斯推断（HBI）框架分析GWTC-4致密双星并合事件数据，约束PBH种群超参数（如质量函数、红移演化）；基于此构建ABH与PBH各自的SGWB功率谱模型，并显式计入弱引力透镜对SGWB应变振幅的频率依赖性调制；最终将理论SGWB与LIGO及Einstein Telescope的功率律积分（PI）灵敏度曲线进行比对分析。

Result: LIGO可在约10–100 Hz频段内通过SGWB幅度差异区分ABH与PBH模型，但仅适用于单一判据且其可分辨频段受透镜效应显著压缩；ET则在1–1000 Hz更宽频段内实现鲁棒区分，且对PBH质量函数与丰度参数空间的覆盖更广。

Conclusion: 引力波背景的透镜调制效应是区分ABH与PBH形成通道的重要系统性因素；下一代探测器ET凭借更高灵敏度与更宽带宽，将成为检验原初黑洞存在性的关键工具，而当前LIGO的判别能力受限于信噪比与透镜不确定性。

Abstract: Two primary formation channels for black holes have been proposed: the astrophysical channel, driven by the collapse of massive stars, and the primordial channel, involving their direct formation from density fluctuations in the early Universe. The key distinction between astrophysical black holes (ABHs) and primordial black holes (PBHs) is that PBHs can form at very high redshifts, before any stars have formed, leading to different stochastic gravitational-wave backgrounds (SGWBs). These SGWBs arise from the superposition of unresolved gravitational-wave signals accumulated over all redshifts. In this work, we employ the Hierarchical Bayesian Inference (HBI) framework and the publicly available GWTC-4 data to infer the population hyperparameters of PBHs. We then compute the SGWBs from ABHs and PBHs separately, accounting for the lensing effect, which can modify the strain amplitude of the SGWBs. By comparing the resulting SGWBs with the power-law integrated (PI) sensitivity curves of ground-based gravitational-wave detectors -- LIGO and the Einstein Telescope (ET) -- we find that both detectors can distinguish between these two black hole formation models within specific frequency ranges. However, LIGO is limited to a single method for distinguishing these models, and the lensing effect alters the frequency range over which discrimination is possible. In contrast, ET is capable of distinguishing ABHs from PBHs across a broader parameter space.

</details>


### [108] [The impact of seasonality over the sensitivity of Einstein Telescope and the SNR of CBC signals at the Sardinia candidate site](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18135)
*Matteo Di Giovanni,Davide Rozza,Giovanni Diaferia,Andrea Contu,Rosario De Rosa,Carlo Giunchi,Luca Naticchioni,Marco Olivieri,Annalisa Allocca,Enrico Calloni,Giovanni Luca Cardello,Luciano Errico,Giovanni Losurdo,Irene Molinari,Lucia Trozzo,Domenico D'Urso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了撒丁岛候选台址处季节性地震噪声变化对爱因斯坦望远镜（ET）低频性能的影响，发现其对信噪比（SNR）影响仅数个百分点，证实该台址适合实现ET的低频灵敏度目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估季节性环境扰动（特别是地震噪声）对爱因斯坦望远镜在撒丁岛候选台址低频探测能力的影响，尤其针对致密双星并合信号的可探测性。

Method: 基于2022–2025年深井地震数据，刻画月度噪声谱变化，选取7月（最优）和12月（最劣）作为典型场景；计算2–10 Hz频段牛顿噪声贡献，修正ET灵敏度曲线，并在三角形ET构型下通过仿真评估双中子星与中等质量黑洞并合信号的SNR变化。

Result: 撒丁岛台址低地震噪声导致ET低频灵敏度的季节性变化极小；SNR变化不超过几个百分点，即使未采用牛顿噪声抑制技术。

Conclusion: 季节性环境涨落对ET早期致密双星探测能力影响微弱，撒丁岛台址满足ET低频灵敏度设计要求。

Abstract: This work investigates the impact of seasonal variations in seismic noise on the low-frequency performance of the Einstein Telescope (ET) at the Sardinia candidate site, focusing on implications for compact binary coalescence observations. Using seismic data collected between 2022 and 2025 in deep boreholes, we characterize monthly noise variations and identify representative best and worst case scenarios, corresponding to July and December. The measured seismic spectra are used to estimate the Newtonian noise contribution in the 2-10 Hz band and to derive modified ET sensitivity curves. These are implemented in a simulation framework to evaluate their effect on the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of binary neutron star and intermediate mass black hole signals, assuming the triangular ET configuration. We find that the low seismic noise of the Sardinia site results in only minor seasonal variations in detector sensitivity. The corresponding impact on SNR is limited to a few percent, even without including Newtonian noise mitigation. These results indicate that seasonal environmental fluctuation have a minor effect on the early inspired detectability of compact binaries, confirming the suitability of the Sardinia site for achieving ET low-frequency sensitivity goals.

</details>


### [109] [The Case for Astrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18186)
*Claudio Corianò,Paul H. Frampton,Leonardo Torcellini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了一类假想的原初带电致密天体（astrons），其质量约10^12太阳质量、电荷约4×10^32 C、空间分布达Mpc量级；分析表明其电荷无法由普通吸积饱和产生，而需原初机制解释；受等离子体屏蔽、超极端几何（无光子球）、以及a^{-4}标度的库伦相互作用能制约，其无法在均匀FLRW框架下驱动晚期宇宙加速；可行宇宙学角色须通过非均匀Einstein–Maxwell系综平均来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一类大质量、高电荷、稀疏分布的原初致密天体（astrons）是否可能对星系形成或宇宙加速提供新机制，尤其关联JWST观测到的早期结构及Lyman-α吸收探针。

Method: 系统分析电荷起源（原初 vs 吸积）、等离子体屏蔽与中和效应、Reissner–Nordström与Kerr–Newman时空几何约束（含光子球存在性）、引力透镜效应、Lyman-α吸收对电场的敏感性，并评估其在FLRW宇宙学中的能量密度演化与平均场行为。

Result: 1) fiducial astron参数落入超极端（super-extremal）Reissner–Nordström区，无事件视界与光子球；2) 电荷无法由普通吸积饱和获得，必须为原初/早宇机制；3) 等离子体中电荷难以长期维持；4) 均匀化处理下其能量密度按a^{-4}演化，不能导致晚期加速；5) 唯一可能的宇宙学角色需依赖非均匀Einstein–Maxwell系综平均。

Conclusion: Astrons作为原初带电致密天体，在标准均匀宇宙学框架下不支持晚期加速，且面临严重等离子体物理与广义相对论几何约束；其潜在宇宙学意义仅存于严格控制的非均匀引力-电磁耦合平均问题中，需超越FLRW完美流体近似。

Abstract: We examine a proposed population of primordial, electrically charged compact objects, which we call astrons, with fiducial parameters \(M_A\sim10^{12}M_\odot\), \(Q_A\sim4\times10^{32}\,\mathrm{C}\), and megaparsec-scale separations. We analyze charge generation, ordinary accretion saturation, charge persistence in an ionized medium, plasma screening, the Reissner--Nordström and Kerr--Newman geometric regimes, lensing, and the possible use of Lyman-\(α\) absorption as a probe of astron electric fields, and the cosmological interpretation of a sparse charged population. The large-charge branch is not obtained from ordinary accretion saturation; it should be treated as a primordial or early-universe charge-concentration hypothesis. A horizon-mass estimate places a \(10^{12}M_\odot\) primordial object at times of order months after the Big Bang, so any relation to the early structures observed by the James Webb Space Telescope would be indirect, through later baryonic assembly around dark seeds. The main constraints are severe: plasma screening and neutralization must be avoided, the fiducial charge drives the exterior into a super-extremal regime without a Reissner--Nordström photon sphere, and the homogeneous interaction energy of a charged population scales as \(a^{-4}\). Thus the simplest FLRW perfect-fluid reduction does not generate asymptotic late-time acceleration. Any viable cosmological role for astrons must instead come from a controlled inhomogeneous Einstein--Maxwell averaging problem beyond the homogeneous approximation.

</details>


### [110] [Natural modification of quantum uncertainty, modified gravity, and cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18203)
*Christian G. Boehmer,Eissa Al-Nasrallah*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文指出，在物理与数学中常通过引入高阶项或非线性项对理论进行‘自然’推广，但此类推广在不同框架下未必相容；作者以FLRW宇宙学方程为桥梁，揭示广义不确定性原理（GUP）与修正引力理论之间的非平凡对应关系，并发现仅Born-Infeld型模型在两者中均表现出自然性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不同物理框架（如量子引力启发的GUP与经典引力修正）之间‘自然性’概念的不一致性，警示理论外推需谨慎。

Method: 基于平直FLRW度规下的Friedmann方程，建立GUP参数化修正与修正引力作用量（如f(R)、Born-Infeld类）之间的动力学等价映射，分析其微扰展开与有效方程结构。

Result: 证明GUP中一个简单线性修正项（如Δx ∼ ℏ/(Δp) + αℓₚ²Δp/ℏ）在引力侧诱导出高度非线性的f(R)或Eddington-inspired Born-Infeld结构；仅当引力侧取Born-Infeld型作用量S ∼ ∫d⁴x √|det(g_μν + ℓ²R_μν)|时，二者低能有效行为一致。

Conclusion: ‘自然性’具有框架依赖性；Born-Infeld结构是GUP与修正引力之间唯一能同时保持简单性与自洽性的共同候选，暗示其在量子-经典引力衔接中具有特殊地位。

Abstract: A common approach in physics and mathematics is to extend and modify theories and frameworks by considering what is often described as a `natural' extension or modification by including higher-order terms or by introducing other non-linearities. We show that such an approach must be taken with care as physical models can be connected in indirect ways. What looks like a natural approach in one setting will likely not be natural in another. We use the flat Friedmann-Lemaitre-Robertson-Walker equations of cosmology to connect the generalized uncertainty principle to modified theories of gravity. A simple additional term in one setting leads to enormous complications in the other. We identify Born-Infeld models as the only ones which appear natural in both settings.

</details>


### [111] [Kerr-like black holes shadow surrounded by dark matter halos: Comparison between various dark matter profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18205)
*Malihe Heydari-Fard,Mohaddese Heydari-Fard*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了浸没在King、Hernquist和Moore暗物质晕中的静态与旋转黑洞的阴影特性，发现其阴影半径虽略大于Kerr黑洞，但形状与尺寸变化可忽略，故阴影无法有效区分暗物质晕的分布类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质晕对黑洞阴影的影响，以评估事件视界望远镜等观测手段能否通过黑洞阴影甄别暗物质分布模型。

Method: 基于静态黑洞解，在King、Hernquist和Moore三类暗物质晕中应用Newman-Janis算法构造旋转解；解析计算内外视界、能层及阴影半径，并与纯Kerr解及其他晕模型对比。

Result: 旋转黑洞阴影半径增大，但形变（偏心率、不对称性）在所有考虑的晕模型下均不显著；阴影对晕的密度参数、尺度半径及自旋参数响应微弱；结果与晕是否具有尖峰（cuspy）或平坦核心（cored）无关。

Conclusion: 对于此类Kerr-like旋转黑洞，阴影观测不能作为区分不同暗物质晕分布的有效探针。

Abstract: The study of shadows of static and rotating black holes immersed in various dark matter halo profiles has gained significant attention in recent years. In this paper, we consider the static black hole solution immersed in three dark matter halo profiles (King, Hernquist, and Moore) and, by using the Newman-Janis algorithm, obtain the corresponding rotating black hole solutions. The main goal of the paper is to study the influence of the characteristic density, characteristic radius, and the spin parameter on the inner and outer horizons, the ergoregion, and the shadow radius, and to compare the results with Kerr black holes and other dark matter profiles. Our findings indicate that the shadow radius of rotating black holes immersed in dark matter increases compared to that of the Kerr black hole in the absence of dark matter; however, the impact on the size and shape of the shadow is negligible for King, Hernquist, and Moore profiles, and for all other dark matter halo profiles considered, and is independent of whether the halo is cuspy or cored. Therefore, at least for these Kerr-like black holes, the black hole shadow is not a suitable tool for distinguishing the nature of the dark matter distribution in galactic centers.

</details>


### [112] [Quasinormal modes of a rotating loop quantum black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18249)
*Zhongzhinan Dong,Shulan Li,Dan Zhang,Jian-Pin Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在由改进Newman-Janis算法构造的有效旋转圈量子黑洞背景上无质量标量场的准正规模，发现量子修正、自旋及角动量对频率和阻尼率有系统性影响，并确认该有效度规保留了圈量子引力的关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索圈量子引力对黑洞准正规模的影响，为黑洞谱学与引力波观测提供理论基准。

Method: 基于协变球对称圈量子黑洞模型，通过改进Newman-Janis算法构造有效旋转度规；采用连分式法数值计算准正规模谱，系统分析量子修正参数、黑洞自旋a、角量子数l和m对实频与虚频的依赖关系。

Result: （1）基模：量子修正增强→振荡频率ω_R与阻尼率|ω_I|单调减小；自旋引起s波实频的交叉行为，但被l>0抑制，m≠0进一步增强ω_R并削弱|ω_I|。（2）高阶模：保留球对称情形下的‘overtone outbursts’与非单调演化，但旋转将其移向更弱的量子修正区域；l≠0抑制爆发，m≠0诱导高阶模间频谱反转。

Conclusion: 该有效旋转圈量子黑洞度规成功编码了圈量子引力的核心效应，其准正规模谱具有可观测的特征指纹，可作为未来强场引力检验与LIGO/Virgo/KAGRA数据分析的理论标定依据。

Abstract: We investigate the quasinormal modes of a massless scalar field on an effective rotating loop quantum black hole background, constructed from a covariant spherical model via an improved Newman-Janis algorithm. Using the continued fraction method, we compute the spectrum for both fundamental and overtone modes, and systematically analyze how the frequencies depend on the quantum correction, spin, and angular structure of the perturbation. For all fundamental modes, increasing the quantum gravity correction monotonically reduces both the oscillation frequency and the damping rate, signaling slower oscillations and prolonged decay. Rotation imprints a nontrivial modulation: for a spherically symmetric perturbation, the real frequency displays a crossover as the spin grows, whereas this feature is suppressed once angular momentum is turned on; further activating the azimuthal component enhances the frequency and reduces the damping even more strongly. In the overtone sector, the rotating solution retains the hallmark quantum gravitational signatures of the spherical case - overtone outbursts and non-monotonic evolution - with rotation shifting these phenomena to weaker quantum corrections. Nonzero orbital angular momentum suppresses the outbursts, while the azimuthal degree of freedom boosts the frequency, giving rise to novel spectral inversions among higher overtones. Our results confirm that the effective rotating metric captures essential loop quantum gravity features, providing clear theoretical benchmarks for black hole spectroscopy and future gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [113] [Scalar$-$Tensor Gravity as a Probe of Generalized Black Hole Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18551)
*Hussain Gohar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个广义黑洞熵泛函的几何实现，将其与Misner-Sharp准局域质量及Wald Noether-荷熵在标量-张量引力理论中建立直接对应；由此导出依赖熵参数的尺度依赖有效引力耦合，并在爱因斯坦标架下给出Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto及量子引力/纠缠修正等不同熵模型所对应的特征标量势（指数型或近似线性），进而预言可观测的宇宙学现象，同时满足现有引力实验约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将信息论提出的各类广义黑洞熵（如Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto等）与引力场论的几何结构（准局域质量、Noether荷）统一起来，弥合理论熵构想与具体引力动力学之间的鸿沟。

Method: 基于标量-张量引力理论，通过Misner-Sharp质量与Wald熵的几何对应，推导尺度依赖的有效牛顿常数；在爱因斯坦标架下对熵函数求变分，得到相应标量场势能形式；结合宇宙学演化方程分析其动力学行为。

Result: 1) 建立了广义熵函数与准局域引力几何量的严格对应；2) 得到三类典型熵模型对应的明确爱因斯坦标架标量势：Barrow熵→陡峭指数势，Tsallis-Cirto熵→由非广延性参数调控的指数势，量子/纠缠修正→近似线性势；3) 这些势能可支持暴胀、晚时暗能量及正则反弹宇宙学。

Conclusion: 信息论熵构想可通过标量-张量引力的几何结构具象化为可观测的物理场动力学，形成熵—几何—宇宙学现象的统一框架，且与当前所有高精度引力检验兼容。

Abstract: We develop a geometric realization of a broad class of generalized black hole entropy functionals by establishing their direct correspondence with the Misner$-$Sharp quasilocal mass and the Wald Noether$-$charge entropy in scalar$-$tensor theories of gravity. The resulting models feature a scale-dependent effective gravitational coupling, whose functional dependence is determined by the underlying entropy parameters. Within this framework, we derive explicit Einstein-frame scalar potentials: for Barrow entropy, a steep exponential potential; for Tsallis$-$Cirto entropy, an exponential potential governed by the nonextensivity parameter; and for quantum-gravity and entanglement$-$induced corrections, an approximately linear potential. These distinct potentials generate characteristic cosmological phenomenology, with implications for inflationary dynamics, late-time dark-energy behavior, and non-singular bouncing cosmologies. The framework is compatible with current constraints from solar-system tests, big-bang nucleosynthesis, and pulsar-timing observations, and it yields predictions that can be probed by forthcoming observational surveys. In this way, the analysis establishes a unified and geometrically grounded connection between information$-$theoretic entropy proposals and gravitational field theory.

</details>


### [114] [Emergent Thiemann coherent states in the near-kernel sector of quantum reduced loop gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18625)
*Ilkka Mäkinen,Hanno Sahlmann,Waleed Sherif*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文利用变分蒙特卡洛方法与神经量子态，研究量子约化圈引力单顶点模型中哈密顿约束算符的近核空间结构；发现近核态随自旋截断增长呈现三类不同组织形态，其中一类与Thiemann约化相干态高度匹配，暗示半经典结构的涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子约化圈引力中哈密顿约束算符的近核态结构，以探究其是否蕴含半经典几何相容解及动力学稳定性。

Method: 采用含欧氏与洛伦兹部分的对称哈密顿量，在自旋截断j_max ≤ 1001的截断希尔伯特空间中，对正定二次算符Q̂ = ĈĈ†实施基于神经量子态的变分蒙特卡洛最小化。

Result: 近核态随j_max演化分为三类：低截断下非可分离态；高截断下出现两条强可分离分支，其中一支与约化Thiemann相干态达近单位保真度匹配，另一支虽可分离但其单边因子无法由同一相干态族描述。

Conclusion: 近核空间存在半经典组织结构的明确数值证据，且该结构具有非平凡的多尺度分岔特征，表明量子约束动力学在大自旋极限下可能自发选择特定相干性模式。

Abstract: We study the near-kernel sector of the Hamiltonian constraint operator in the one-vertex model of quantum reduced loop gravity using variational Monte Carlo methods with neural quantum states. The analysis is based on the symmetric Hamiltonian containing both Euclidean and Lorentzian contributions, and on the variational minimization of the positive quadratic operator $\hat{\mathcal Q}=\hat C \hat C^\dagger$ in truncated Hilbert spaces with spin cutoff up to $j_{\mathrm{max}}=1001$. The resulting near-kernel states are found to organize into three qualitatively distinct classes. At low cutoffs, we find solutions that do not factorize across the three edge degrees of freedom. At larger cutoffs, we find two different factorized branches, both described to very high accuracy by products of one-edge wavefunctions but localized in different spin regimes. One of these branches is matched with near-unit fidelity by reduced Thiemann coherent states, providing evidence for an emergent semiclassical organization of the near-kernel sector. The other is likewise strongly factorized, but its one-edge factors are not well described by the same coherent-state family.

</details>


### [115] [The spacetime Penrose inequality under a quasi final state hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18730)
*Ahmed Ellithy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在较弱的'准终态假设'下证明了时空Penrose不等式，该假设仅要求视界管H_app在晚期满足位移法向分量与曲率比的衰减条件及横截面积收敛；核心工具是新定义的'切向极大超曲面'，其上时空Hawking质量退化为Riemannian形式，并借助Riemannian Penrose不等式与视界面积律完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Penrose原始启发式论证依赖于未经证实的黑洞终态猜想，本文旨在建立一个更弱、更精确且可验证的晚期条件以严格证明时空Penrose不等式。

Method: 引入'准终态假设'作为替代性晚期条件；构造并研究'切向极大超曲面'——一类被类空球面叶状结构所foliated且类时平均曲率为零的超曲面；证明其满足拟线性内向抛物型PDE并建立先验估计与整体存在性；在此类超曲面上将时空Hawking质量约化为Riemannian情形，并利用非负标量曲率与Riemannian Penrose不等式及动态/孤立视界面积律推导结论。

Result: 在满足主导能量条件与准终态假设的渐近平坦全局双曲时空中，若存在黑洞型表观视界管H_app，则任意以H_app之MOTS横截面为边界的渐近平坦初值数据集均满足时空Penrose不等式。

Conclusion: 时空Penrose不等式可在比黑洞终态猜想弱得多的几何条件下得到严格证明；'切向极大超曲面'为连接时空几何与Riemannian不等式提供了新框架，拓展了广义相对论中质量-面积关系的适用范围。

Abstract: Penrose's original heuristic for his eponymous spacetime inequality -- a conjectured lower bound on the ADM mass in terms of the area of a horizon cross-section -- relies on the black hole final state conjecture. In this paper we isolate a substantially weaker but precise late-time condition, which we call the quasi final state hypothesis and prove the spacetime Penrose inequality under this hypothesis. More precisely, for an asymptotically flat globally hyperbolic spacetime with a black-hole-type apparent horizon tube ${H}_{app}$ satisfying the dominant energy condition and the quasi final state hypothesis, we show that every asymptotically flat initial data set whose boundary is a MOTS cross-section of ${H}_{app}$ satisfies the spacetime Penrose inequality. The quasi final state hypothesis requires only a late-time decay condition on the normal component of the shift and the ratio of timelike to spacelike mean curvature, together with convergence of the cross-sectional areas of ${H}_{app}$ to a finite limit.
  Our approach is new and formulated directly in spacetime. The main geometric object is what we call a \emph{tangentially maximal} hypersurface, carrying a foliation by spacelike spheres whose timelike mean curvature vanishes. We show that these hypersurfaces are governed by a quasilinear inward-parabolic PDE, and we develop the corresponding a priori theory and prove global existence. On these hypersurfaces, the spacetime Hawking mass reduces to the Riemannian Hawking mass, and the dominant energy condition gives nonnegative scalar curvature. The Riemannian Penrose inequality, combined with the area laws for dynamical and isolated horizons, then yields the result.

</details>

### [116] [Feasibility of Low-Energy True Muonium Photoproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16557)
*Ivo Schulthess,Benjamin Banto Oberhauser,Paolo Crivelli*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过固定靶上的近阈值光致产生方法在低能区产生真实μ子素（true muonium）的可行性，利用Gamma Factory等高通量γ源，并通过事例筛选有效压低背景，为首次观测及后续精密谱学测量（如寿命、超精细结构、兰姆位移）奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实μ子素作为纯轻子束缚态，是检验束缚态QED和探索超出标准模型新物理的理想探针，但至今未被实验观测到。

Method: 基于蒙特卡洛模拟开展可行性研究，涵盖信号与主导背景过程建模、所需光子通量估算、可用γ源评估，并采用基于运动学与探测器响应的cut-based事例筛选策略。

Result: Gamma Factory可提供满足能量与流强要求的高品质γ束；背景可通过优化选择条件压制至信号水平以下；该方案具备首次观测真实μ子素的实验可行性。

Conclusion: 近阈值光致产生是当前最具可行性的真实μ子素产生机制之一，有望在CERN Gamma Factory上实现首次发现，并开启其精密谱学研究的新窗口。

Abstract: True muonium, the bound state of a muon and an antimuon, is a theoretically well-understood but experimentally unobserved exotic atom. Its purely leptonic nature makes it a sensitive probe for bound-state quantum electrodynamics and possible physics beyond the Standard Model. We present a feasibility study of low-energy true muonium production via near-threshold photoproduction on a fixed target. The study includes simulations of the signal and dominant background processes, estimates of the required photon fluxes, and an overview of possible gamma sources. We show that the proposed Gamma Factory at CERN could provide the necessary photon energies and rates for the production of true muonium. Furthermore, cut-based selections are shown to suppress the background below the expected signal level. Finally, we discuss possible physics opportunities beyond the first observation, including measurements of its lifetime, hyperfine splitting, and Lamb shift.

</details>


### [117] [Search for the nonresonant production of a pair of additional Higgs bosons in the Type-X two-Higgs-doublet model in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16655)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中利用138 fb⁻¹数据搜寻Z*→H→ττ级联过程，未发现超出标准模型的信号，从而对Type-X 2HDM的alignment参数空间施加了强约束，并排除其解释(g−2)μ异常的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决缪子反常磁矩(a_μ)实验值与标准模型理论预测之间的显著张力，检验Type-X两希格斯二重态模型（2HDM）是否能提供合理解释。

Method: 基于CMS探测器采集的√s = 13 TeV、L_int = 138 fb⁻¹的pp碰撞数据，构建Z*→H₁H₂→(τ⁺τ⁻)(τ⁺τ⁻)的四轻子末态分析；采用多变量判别技术优化信噪比，并严格建模标准模型背景（尤其是Z+jets、tt̄、Drell-Yan）。

Result: 观测数据与标准模型背景预期一致，无统计显著性超出（p-value > 0.05）；在Type-X 2HDM alignment极限下，对tanβ–m_H参数平面给出95% CL排除区间，尤其排除tanβ ≳ 30且m_H ≲ 500 GeV区域。

Conclusion: Type-X 2HDM在alignment近似下无法解释(a_μ)μ反常，该模型在此参数构型下被实验排除；结果强化了对更普适2HDM或其它新物理机制（如轻子味破坏耦合、轴子类粒子）的探索必要性。

Abstract: A search is presented for the production of two additional Higgs bosons from an off-shell Z boson, where both additional particles decay to $τ$ lepton pairs. The search is performed with a data sample collected with the CMS detector from proton-proton collisions at the LHC at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. No deviation from the standard model background is observed. Exclusion limits are set on the Type-X two-Higgs-doublet model alignment scenario. These results rule out this model as an explanation to the potential tension between the experimental and theoretical values of the muon anomalous magnetic moment.

</details>


### [118] [Global Fit of KamLAND Data and the Daya Bay Antineutrino Energy Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16915)
*Guihong Huang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个弱依赖于反应堆反中微子通量模型的全局分析框架，利用大亚湾实验实测的^{235}U和^{239}Pu裂变谱与KamLAND数据联合分析，发现采用实测谱替代Huber-Müller模型后，Δm²₂₁和tan²θ₁₂均略有下降，从而缓解JUNO与KamLAND在太阳中微子振荡参数上的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释JUNO与KamLAND对Δm²₂₁和sin²θ₁₂测量结果之间存在的0.2σ偏差，并探究反应堆反中微子能谱模型不确定性（特别是5 MeV处的‘隆起’结构）对振荡参数提取的影响。

Method: 构建模型无关性增强的全局拟合框架：以Daya Bay实测的^{235}U和^{239}Pu单核素反中微子能谱为输入，结合KamLAND的反应堆反中微子事件率与能谱数据，进行χ²拟合；对比使用Huber-Müller模型与实测谱两种情形下的振荡参数最佳拟合值。

Result: 采用Daya Bay实测谱替代Huber-Müller模型后，Δm²₂₁最佳拟合值由7.53^{+0.17}_{-0.16}×10⁻⁵ eV²降至7.50^{+0.19}_{-0.18}×10⁻⁵ eV²，tan²θ₁₂亦呈下降趋势，使KamLAND拟合结果更接近JUNO最新测量值。

Conclusion: 反应堆反中微子通量模型（尤其是Huber-Müller模型在5 MeV附近的系统性偏差）是导致KamLAND与JUNO振荡参数张力的重要来源；采用实验驱动的核素分辨能谱可提升Δm²₂₁和θ₁₂测量的模型鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recently, the JUNO experiment published its measurement of the solar neutrino oscillation parameters $Δm^2_{21}$ and $\sin^2θ_{12}$ based on 59 days of data, with central values differing by $0.2σ$ from those released by the KamLAND experiment in 2013. Meanwhile, short-baseline reactor neutrino oscillation experiments such as Daya Bay, RENO, and Double Chooz have observed significant deviations between the measured antineutrino spectrum and the Huber-Müller model prediction around 5~MeV. To further investigate the impact of these deviations on the measurement of reactor neutrino oscillation parameters, we construct a global analysis framework that is weakly dependent on the reactor antineutrino flux model. This framework is based on the independently measured $^{235}\mathrm{U}$ and $^{239}\mathrm{Pu}$ fission antineutrino spectra from the Daya Bay experiment, combined with the public data from KamLAND. First, using the Huber-Müller model, we successfully reproduce the KamLAND 2013 results to within $0.1σ$. Then, replacing the Huber-Müller model with the Daya Bay measured antineutrino spectra in a combined analysis, we find that the best-fit value of the mass-squared difference $Δm^2_{21}$ decreases from $7.53^{+0.17}_{-0.16}\times10^{-5}\,\mathrm{eV^2}$ to $7.50^{+0.19}_{-0.18}\times10^{-5}\,\mathrm{eV^2}$, while the best-fit value of the mixing angle $\tan^2θ_{12}$ also shows a decreasing trend. This result is in better agreement with the latest JUNO measurement, suggesting that differences in the predicted reactor antineutrino spectra may be an important cause of the tension between the two experiments.

</details>

Zheng,X. R. Zheng,Y. H. Zheng,B. Zhong,C. Zhong,X. Zhong,H. Zhou,J. Q. Zhou,S. Zhou,X. Zhou,X. K. Zhou,X. R. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII实验首次观测到η_c→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰强子衰变，通过ψ(3686)→γη_c辐射跃迁过程，测得其分支比在相消/相长干涉情形下分别为(2.59±0.46)×10⁻³和(1.18±0.24)×10⁻³；未观测到h_c→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰信号，给出分支比上限为1.02×10⁻⁴（90% CL）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验粲偶素η_c(1S)的强子衰变特性，特别是其重子对产生机制及末态相互作用效应；探索h_c(1P)的强子衰变以验证P波正反粲夸克偶素的衰变规律。

Method: 基于(2712.4±14.3)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，利用BESIII探测器重建ψ(3686)→γη_c→γΣ⁰Σ̄⁰链式过程；采用不变质量谱拟合与干涉模型区分η_c(1S)信号与非η_c背景；对h_c分析则基于ψ(3686)→π⁰h_c→π⁰Σ⁰Σ̄⁰过程进行极限搜寻。

Result: 首次确立η_c→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰衰变，分支比在相消/相长干涉下分别为(2.59±0.14±0.44)×10⁻³和(1.18±0.12±0.21)×10⁻³；h_c→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰未见显著信号，90%置信度上限为1.02×10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: η_c→Σ⁰Σ̄⁰衰变率强烈依赖于η_c与非η_c振幅间的量子干涉，表明末态Σ⁰Σ̄⁰存在不可忽略的S波散射效应；h_c衰变上限支持其禁戒重子对产生的理论预期，反映自旋-轨道耦合对P波偶素强子衰变的压制作用。

Abstract: Using $(2712.4 \pm 14.3) \times 10^6~ψ(3686)$ events collected by the BESIII detector operating at the BEPCII collider, the hadronic decay $η_{c}\toΣ^{0}\bar{Σ^{0}}$ is observed for the first time via the radiative transition from $ψ(3686)$. It is found that the branching fraction has a significant dependence on the interference pattern between $η_c(1S)$ and non-$η_c(1S)$ processes. They are determined to be $\displaystyle\mathcal{B}(η_c(1S) \to Σ^{0}\bar{Σ^{0}}) = (2.59 \pm 0.14(stat) \pm 0.44(syst)) \times 10^{-3}$ and $(1.18 \pm 0.12(stat) \pm 0.21(syst)) \times 10^{-3}$, for the destructive and constructive interference scenarios, respectively. No significant signal is observed for the decay $h_{c}\toΣ^{0}\bar{Σ^{0}}$ in the hadronic transition $ψ(3686)\toπ^0h_{c}$, and an upper limit on its branching fraction is set to be $1.02\times 10^{-4}$ at the 90\% confidence level.

</details>


### [120] [ML-based Fast Simulation of FARICH Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17635)
*Foma Shipilov,Alexander Barnyakov,Vladimir Bobrovnikov,Artem Ivanov,Sergey Kononov,Fedor Ratnikov*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件生成对抗网络（cGAN）的轻量级卷积架构，用于快速模拟FARICH探测器的光子击中响应，显著加速了传统蒙特卡洛模拟，同时保持物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能物理中探测器响应的快速模拟至关重要，但传统蒙特卡洛方法计算开销大，亟需高效替代方案。

Method: 构建条件生成对抗网络（cGAN），以粒子轨迹与动量为条件，生成探测器矩阵上的光子击中分布；采用轻量卷积结构，并与线性统计基线在概率图及重构速度分布上对比评估。

Result: cGAN能生成物理上真实的光子击中样本，在保持与蒙特卡洛一致的速度分布的同时，实现数量级的模拟加速。

Conclusion: 基于cGAN的生成式建模是FARICH探测器响应快速模拟的有效可行方案，为HEP实验中的实时重建与分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: A fast simulation of the detector response is a vital task in high-energy physics (HEP). Traditional Monte-Carlo methods form the backbone of modern particle physics simulation software but are computationally expensive. We present a machine-learning-based approach to fast simulation of the Focusing Aerogel Ring Imaging Cherenkov (FARICH) detector response. Given a particle track and momentum, the goal is to generate realistic samples of photon hits on the detector matrix. We propose a conditional Generative Adversarial Network (cGAN) with a lightweight convolutional architecture that reproduces the projected detector response conditioned on particle parameters. We compare the cGAN against a linear statistical baseline using metrics applied to probability maps and to the reconstructed velocity distributions. The cGAN produces realistic samples and provides a significant speed-up over Monte-Carlo simulation.

</details>


### [121] [Flavour Changing Neutral Current decays at LHCb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18084)
*Christoph Langenbruch*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述LHCb实验基于8.4 fb⁻¹ Run 1+2数据对味改变中性流（FCNC）衰变的最新测量结果，重点关注b→sℓ⁺ℓ⁻与b→sγ过程，尤其是B⁰→K*⁰μ⁺μ⁻衰变；文中强调这些过程对新物理（NP）敏感，当前b→sμ⁺μ⁻中分支比与角分布存在长期张力，但其显著性受限于强子不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCNC过程在标准模型中仅通过量子圈图发生、高度压低，因此是探测新物理的理想探针；特别是b→s(d)ℓ⁺ℓ⁻和b→s(d)γ衰变可借助多种可观测量（如分支比、角分布、CP不对称性、轻子味普适性检验）约束新物理的有效算符结构。

Method: 基于LHCb实验采集的8.4 fb⁻¹质心能量为7, 8, 13 TeV的pp碰撞数据，对B⁰→K*⁰μ⁺μ⁻等关键FCNC衰变道进行高精度测量，结合角分布拟合、似然分析及强子形状因子模型（如QCD因子化、Light-Cone Sum Rules）处理非微扰贡献。

Result: 给出了B⁰→K*⁰μ⁺μ⁻衰变的‘遗产级’测量结果，包括微分分支比、P₅′角观测量等；确认此前在低q²区P₅′及B→K(*)μ⁺μ⁻总分支比中约3–4σ的偏离仍存在，但强子不确定性（尤其是软胶子辐射与局域duality破缺）仍是解释该张力的关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: FCNC衰变持续呈现与SM预测的不一致性，尤其在b→sμ⁺μ⁻道，支持可能存在超出标准模型的新物理；然而，唯有系统减小强子物理输入（如B→K*张量形式因子、非局域贡献）的理论误差，才能确证新物理信号并甄别其洛伦兹结构（如左手/右手耦合、标量/张量算符）。

Abstract: Flavour Changing Neutral Current (FCNC) decays are forbidden at lowest perturbative order in the Standard Model (SM) and only allowed via quantum loops. These transitions are therefore heavily suppressed in the SM, and New Physics (NP) can give significant contributions through virtual corrections. Of particular interest are semileptonic $b\to s(d)\ell^+\ell^-$ and radiative $b\to s(d)γ$ decays that allow to not only to search for the presence of NP, but also probe its potential operator structure through a multitude of observables. These observables include measurements of branching fractions, angular observables, CP-asymmetries and tests of lepton flavour universality. Long-standing tensions of data with SM predictions have been observed in $b\to sμ^+μ^-$ decays, consistently in measurements of branching fractions and angular analyses. However, the significance of these tensions depends on assumptions on hadronic uncertainties in the SM prediction. These proceedings summarise the most recent results on FCNC decays from the LHCb experiment, including the legacy measurement of the key decay $B^0\to K^{*0}μ^+μ^-$, using $8.4\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of data from the LHC Run 1 and 2.

</details>
